Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Pass sender name to LLM for all channel types

## Context

GitHub issue #713: When Moltis receives messages from Telegram (or Discord, WhatsApp, etc.),
the LLM cannot identify who sent the message. All channel handlers already extract `sender_name`
and `username` into `ChannelMessageMeta`, and this metadata is persisted in the session JSON.
However, `ChatMessage::User` has no `name` field, so the identity is silently dropped when
converting persisted messages to typed `ChatMessage`s for the LLM.

## Approach

Add an optional `name` field to `ChatMessage::User` and thread it through both conversion paths:
1. **History path**: `values_to_chat_messages()` extracts `sender_name`/`username` from persisted
   channel metadata.
2. **Current message path**: `run_streaming()`/`run_with_tools()` receive the sender name from
   `params["channel"]` and set it on the new user message.

The OpenAI Chat Completions API natively supports `"name"` on user messages.
Anthropic doesn't have a `name` field, so we prepend `[sender_name]: ` to the content text.

## Files to modify

### 1. `crates/agents/src/model.rs` — Core type + serialization

**a) Add `name` field to `ChatMessage::User` (line 51):**
```rust
User {
    content: UserContent,
    name: Option<String>,
},
```

**b) Update convenience constructors `user()` and `user_multimodal()` (lines 94-105):**
Keep existing constructors with `name: None` for backward compatibility.
Add new constructors:
```rust
pub fn user_named(content: impl Into<String>, name: impl Into<String>) -> Self { ... }
pub fn user_multimodal_named(parts: Vec<ContentPart>, name: impl Into<String>) -> Self { ... }
```

**c) Update `to_openai_value()` (line 141-163):**
Include `"name"` in the JSON when `name.is_some()`:
```rust
ChatMessage::User { content, name } => {
    let mut msg = match content { ... };
    if let Some(n) = name { msg["name"] = serde_json::Value::String(n.clone()); }
    msg
}
```

**d) Update `values_to_chat_messages()` (line 235):**
Extract sender name from the persisted `channel` field:
```rust
"user" => {
    let sender_name = val.get("channel")
        .and_then(|ch| ch["sender_name"].as_str()
            .or_else(|| ch["username"].as_str()))
        .map(|s| s.to_string());
    // ... existing content handling ...
    // Set name on created ChatMessage
}
```

### 2. `crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` — Anthropic format (lines 396-419)

Prepend `[name]: ` to user content text when name is present:
```rust
ChatMessage::User { content, name } => {
    let prefix = name.as_ref().map(|n| format!("[{n}]: ")).unwrap_or_default();
    match content {
        UserContent::Text(text) => {
            let content_str = format!("{prefix}{text}");
            out.push(json!({"role": "user", "content": content_str}));
        },
        // Similar for multimodal — prepend to first text block
    }
}
```

These files pattern-match on `ChatMessage::User { content }` and need the new field:

| File | Line | Action |
|------|------|--------|

| `crates/providers/src/genai_provider.rs` | 64 | Add `name: _` |

| `crates/providers/src/local_llm/models/chat_templates.rs` | 42 | Add `name: _` |

### 4. `crates/chat/src/streaming.rs` — Current message name (line 163)

Pass sender name to the current `ChatMessage::User` construction:
```rust
messages.push(ChatMessage::User {
    content: user_content.clone(),
    name: sender_name.clone(),
});
```

This requires threading `sender_name: Option<String>` through `run_streaming()` and
`run_with_tools()` function signatures.

### 5. `crates/chat/src/service/chat_impl/send.rs` — Extract sender name from params

At the call site (around line 749), extract sender name from channel metadata:
```rust
let sender_name = params.get("channel")
    .and_then(|ch| ch["sender_name"].as_str()
        .or_else(|| ch["username"].as_str()))
    .map(|s| s.to_string());
```
Pass it down to `run_streaming()`/`run_with_tools()`.

### 6. Agent runner paths — `crates/agents/src/runner/{streaming,non_streaming}.rs`

Update `ChatMessage::User { content }` constructions (lines 94, 106) to include `name: None`.
These are agent-loop entries where user content comes from the agent framework, not channels.

### 7. Other match sites

- `crates/agents/src/silent_turn.rs` — pattern matches (add `name: _`)
- `crates/chat/src/compaction_run/test_support.rs` — test pattern matches
- `crates/swift-bridge/src/chat.rs` and `ffi_sessions.rs` — constructions (add `name: None`)
- All test files in `crates/agents/src/model.rs`, `crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs`, runner tests

## Out of scope

- **Web chat sender identity**: Web chat doesn't use `ChannelMessageMeta`. Supporting username
  display for web sessions would require auth context, which is a separate concern.
- **`genai` crate name support**: The `genai` crate abstraction may not support `name`.
  We'll ignore it for now (`name: _`).

## Verification

1. `cargo check` — confirms all pattern matches compile
2. `cargo test` — existing tests pass (constructors with `name: None` are backward-compatible)
3. `just lint` — clippy clean
4. Manual test: Send a Telegram group message, verify the LLM response references the sender
5. Check persisted session JSON to confirm `channel.sender_name` is present
6. Add unit tests in `model.rs`:
   - `values_to_chat_messages` extracts name from channel metadata
   - `to_openai_value` includes `name` field when present, omits when `None`
   - New `user_named()` constructor

```
   211→/// Convert persisted JSON messages (from session store) to typed `ChatMessage`s.
   212→///
   213→/// Skips messages that don't have a valid `role` field, logging a warning.
   214→/// Metadata fields (`created_at`, `model`, `provider`, `inputTokens`,
   215→/// `outputTokens`, `channel`) are silently dropped — they only exist in
   216→/// the persisted JSON, not in `ChatMessage`.
   217→pub fn values_to_chat_messages(values: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<ChatMessage> {
   218→    let mut messages = Vec::with_capacity(values.len());
   219→    // Track tool_call IDs emitted by assistant messages so we only include
   220→    // tool/tool_result messages that have a matching assistant tool_call.
   221→    // Orphan tool results (e.g. from explicit /sh commands) would cause
   222→    // provider API errors.
   223→    let mut pending_tool_call_ids: std::collections::HashSet<String> =
   224→        std::collections::HashSet::new();
   225→    for (i, val) in values.iter().enumerate() {
   226→        let Some(role) = val["role"].as_str() else {
   227→            tracing::warn!(index = i, "skipping message with missing/invalid role");
   228→            continue;
   229→        };
   230→        match role {
   231→            "system" => {
   232→                let content = val["content"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   233→                messages.push(ChatMessage::system(content));
   234→            },
   235→            "user" => {
   236→                let document_context = val["documents"].as_array().and_then(|documents| {
   237→                    let mut sections = Vec::new();
   238→                    for document in documents {
   239→                        let Some(display_name) = document["display_name"].as_str() else {
   240→                            continue;
   241→                        };
   242→                        let Some(mime_type) = document["mime_type"].as_str() else {
   243→                            continue;
   244→                        };
   245→                        let Some(media_ref) = document["media_ref"].as_str() else {
   246→                            continue;
   247→                        };
   248→                        let absolute_path = document_absolute_path_from_media_ref(media_ref);
   249→                        sections.push(format!(
   250→                            "filename: {display_name}\nmime_type: {mime_type}\nlocal_path: {absolute_path}\nmedia_ref: {media_ref}"
   251→                        ));
   252→                    }
   253→                    if sections.is_empty() {
   254→                        None
   255→                    } else {
   256→                        let mut rendered = vec!["[Inbound documents available]".to_string()];
   257→                        rendered.extend(sections);
   258→                        Some(rendered.join("\n\n"))
   259→                    }
   260→                });
   261→
   262→                // Content can be a string or an array (multimodal).
   263→                if let Some(text) = val["content"].as_str() {
   264→                    let content = if let Some(ref document_context) = document_context {
   265→                        if text.trim().is_empty() {
   266→                            document_context.clone()
   267→                        } else {
   268→                            format!("{text}\n\n{document_context}")
   269→                        }
   270→                    } else {
   271→                        text.to_string()
   272→                    };
   273→                    messages.push(ChatMessage::user(content));
   274→                } else if let Some(arr) = val["content"].as_array() {
   275→                    let mut parts: Vec<ContentPart> = arr
   276→                        .iter()
   277→                        .filter_map(|block| {
   278→                            let block_type = block["type"].as_str()?;
   279→                            match block_type {
   280→                                "text" => {
   281→                                    let text = block["text"].as_str()?.to_string();
   282→                                    Some(ContentPart::Text(text))
   283→                                },
   284→                                "image_url" => {
   285→                                    let url = block["image_url"]["url"].as_str()?;
   286→                                    let (media_type, data) = parse_data_uri(url)?;
   287→                                    Some(ContentPart::Image {
   288→                                        media_type: media_type.to_string(),
   289→                                        data: data.to_string(),
   290→                                    })
   291→                                },
   292→                                _ => None,
   293→                            }
   294→                        })
   295→                        .collect();
```

```
   392→/// Events emitted during streaming LLM completion.
   393→#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
   394→pub enum StreamEvent {
   395→    /// Text content delta.
   396→    Delta(String),
   397→    /// Raw provider event payload (for debugging API responses).
   398→    ProviderRaw(serde_json::Value),
   399→    /// Reasoning/planning text delta (not user-visible final answer text).
   400→    ReasoningDelta(String),
   401→    /// A tool call has started (content_block_start with tool_use).
   402→    ToolCallStart {
   403→        /// Tool call ID from the provider.
   404→        id: String,
   405→        /// Tool name being called.
   406→        name: String,
   407→        /// Index of this tool call in the response (0-based).
   408→        index: usize,
   409→    },
   410→    /// Streaming delta for tool call arguments (JSON fragment).
   411→    ToolCallArgumentsDelta {
   412→        /// Index of the tool call this delta belongs to.
   413→        index: usize,
   414→        /// JSON fragment to append to the arguments.
   415→        delta: String,
   416→    },
   417→    /// A tool call's arguments are complete.
   418→    ToolCallComplete {
   419→        /// Index of the completed tool call.
   420→        index: usize,
   421→    },
   422→    /// Stream completed successfully.
   423→    Done(Usage),
   424→    /// An error occurred.
   425→    Error(String),
   426→}
   427→
   428→/// LLM provider trait (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, etc.).
   429→#[async_trait]
   430→pub trait LlmProvider: Send + Sync {

   432→
   433→    /// Model identifier (e.g. "claude-sonnet-4-20250514", "gpt-4o").
   434→    fn id(&self) -> &str;

   445→    fn supports_tools(&self) -> bool {
```

```
   380→/// as a plain string and no `cache_control` blocks are injected.
   381→fn to_anthropic_messages(
   382→    messages: &[ChatMessage],
   383→    caching: bool,
   384→) -> (Option<serde_json::Value>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {
   385→    let mut system_text: Option<String> = None;
   386→    let mut out = Vec::new();
   387→
   388→    for msg in messages {
   389→        match msg {
   390→            ChatMessage::System { content } => {
   391→                system_text = Some(match system_text {
   392→                    Some(existing) => format!("{existing}\n\n{content}"),
   393→                    None => content.clone(),
   394→                });
   395→            },
   396→            ChatMessage::User { content } => match content {
   397→                UserContent::Text(text) => {
   398→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "user", "content": text}));
   399→                },
   400→                UserContent::Multimodal(parts) => {
   401→                    let blocks: Vec<serde_json::Value> = parts
   402→                        .iter()
   403→                        .map(|part| match part {
   404→                            ContentPart::Text(text) => {
   405→                                serde_json::json!({"type": "text", "text": text})
   406→                            },
   407→                            ContentPart::Image { media_type, data } => {
   408→                                serde_json::json!({
   409→                                    "type": "image",
   410→                                    "source": {
   411→                                        "type": "base64",
   412→                                        "media_type": media_type,
   413→                                        "data": data,
   414→                                    }
   415→                                })
   416→                            },
   417→                        })
   418→                        .collect();
   419→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "user", "content": blocks}));
   420→                },
   421→            },
   422→            ChatMessage::Assistant {
   423→                content,
   424→                tool_calls,
   425→            } => {
   426→                if tool_calls.is_empty() {
   427→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({
   428→                        "role": "assistant",
   429→                        "content": content.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
   430→                    }));
   431→                } else {
   432→                    let mut blocks = Vec::new();
   433→                    if let Some(text) = content
   434→                        && !text.is_empty()
   435→                    {
   436→                        blocks.push(serde_json::json!({"type": "text", "text": text}));
   437→                    }
   438→                    for tc in tool_calls {
   439→                        blocks.push(serde_json::json!({
   440→                            "type": "tool_use",
   441→                            "id": tc.id,
   442→                            "name": tc.name,
   443→                            "input": tc.arguments,
   444→                        }));
   445→                    }
   446→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "assistant", "content": blocks}));
   447→                }
   448→            },
   449→            ChatMessage::Tool {
   450→                tool_call_id,
   451→                content,
   452→            } => {
   453→                out.push(serde_json::json!({
   454→                    "role": "user",
   455→                    "content": [{
   456→                        "type": "tool_result",
   457→                        "tool_use_id": tool_call_id,
   458→                        "content": content,
   459→                    }]
```

```
    89→    }
    90→}
    91→
    92→fn message_contains(msg: &ChatMessage, needle: &str) -> bool {
    93→    match msg {
    94→        ChatMessage::System { content } => content.contains(needle),
    95→        ChatMessage::User {
    96→            content: UserContent::Text(t),
    97→        } => t.contains(needle),
    98→        ChatMessage::User {
    99→            content: UserContent::Multimodal(parts),
   100→        } => parts
   101→            .iter()
   102→            .any(|p| matches!(p, ContentPart::Text(t) if t.contains(needle))),
   103→        ChatMessage::Assistant {
   104→            content: Some(text),
   105→            ..
   106→        } => text.contains(needle),
   107→        ChatMessage::Tool { content, .. } => content.contains(needle),
   108→        _ => false,
   109→    }
```

```
    56→const STREAM_SERVER_RETRY_DELAY_MS: u64 = 2_000;
    57→const STREAM_SERVER_MAX_RETRIES: u8 = 1;
    58→const STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_INITIAL_RETRY_MS: u64 = 2_000;
    59→const STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_RETRY_MS: u64 = 60_000;
    60→const STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_RETRIES: u8 = 10;
    61→
    62→fn is_retryable_stream_server_error(msg: &str) -> bool {
    63→    let lower = msg.to_ascii_lowercase();
    64→    STREAM_RETRYABLE_SERVER_PATTERNS
    65→        .iter()
    66→        .any(|pattern| lower.contains(pattern))
    67→}
    68→
    69→fn next_stream_rate_limit_retry_ms(previous_ms: Option<u64>) -> u64 {
    70→    previous_ms
    71→        .map(|ms| ms.saturating_mul(2))
    72→        .unwrap_or(STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_INITIAL_RETRY_MS)
    73→        .clamp(
    74→            STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_INITIAL_RETRY_MS,
    75→            STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_RETRY_MS,
    76→        )
    77→}
    78→
    79→fn next_stream_retry_delay_ms(
    80→    raw_error: &str,
    81→    error_obj: &Value,
    82→    server_retries_remaining: &mut u8,
    83→    rate_limit_retries_remaining: &mut u8,
    84→    rate_limit_backoff_ms: &mut Option<u64>,
    85→) -> Option<u64> {
    86→    if error_obj.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some("rate_limit_exceeded") {
    87→        if *rate_limit_retries_remaining == 0 {
    88→            return None;
    89→        }
    90→        *rate_limit_retries_remaining -= 1;
    91→
    92→        let current_backoff = *rate_limit_backoff_ms;
    93→        *rate_limit_backoff_ms = Some(next_stream_rate_limit_retry_ms(current_backoff));
    94→
    95→        let hinted_ms = error_obj.get("retryAfterMs").and_then(Value::as_u64);
    96→        let delay_ms = hinted_ms
    97→            .or(*rate_limit_backoff_ms)
    98→            .unwrap_or(STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_INITIAL_RETRY_MS);
    99→        return Some(delay_ms.clamp(1, STREAM_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_RETRY_MS));
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    if is_retryable_stream_server_error(raw_error) {
   103→        if *server_retries_remaining == 0 {
   104→            return None;
   105→        }
   106→        *server_retries_remaining -= 1;
   107→        return Some(STREAM_SERVER_RETRY_DELAY_MS);
   108→    }
   109→
   110→    None
   111→}
```

I see — the agent loop function receives `user_content: &UserContent`, not `ChatMessage`. It constructs `ChatMessage::User { content: user_content.clone(), name: None }` internally. For the agent loop, `name: None` is correct since these are agent-framework messages, not channel messages.

For the `run_with_tools` path, the sender name in the *current* message needs to be handled differently. The history path is already handled by `values_to_chat_messages`. For the current message, we need to pass the sender name to `run_with_tools` so it can create a named user message.

Actually, looking at the code flow more carefully:

In `run_with_tools.rs`:

2. It passes `user_content` to `run_agent_loop_streaming` which constructs `ChatMessage::User { content, name: None }`

- Add `sender_name` to `run_with_tools` and thread it down to the agent loop, OR
- Add it to `run_with_tools` and prepend it to history so the agent loop picks it up

\"username\": \"alice42\",\n                \"channel_type\": \"telegram\"\n            }\n        })];\n        let msgs = values_to_chat_messages(&values);\n        assert_eq!(msgs.len(), 1);\n        match &msgs[0] {\n            ChatMessage::User { name, .. } => {\n                assert_eq!(name.as_deref(), Some(\"Alice\"));\n            },\n            _ => panic!(\"expected User message\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn values_to_chat_messages_falls_back_to_username() {\n        let values = vec![serde_json::json!({\n            \"role\": \"user\",\n            \"content\": \"hello\",\n            \"channel\": {\n                \"username\": \"bob99\",\n                \"channel_type\": \"discord\"\n            }\n        })];\n        let msgs = values_to_chat_messages(&values);\n        assert_eq!(msgs.len(), 1);\n        match &msgs[0] {\n            ChatMessage::User { name, .. } => {\n                assert_eq!(name.as_deref(), Some(\"bob99\"));\n            },\n            _ => panic!(\"expected User message\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn values_to_chat_messages_no_channel_means_no_name() {\n        let values = vec![serde_json::json!({\n            \"role\": \"user\",\n            \"content\": \"web message\"\n        })];\n        let msgs = values_to_chat_messages(&values);\n        assert_eq!(msgs.len(), 1);\n        match &msgs[0] {\n            ChatMessage::User { name, .. } => {\n                assert!(name.is_none());\n            },\n            _ => panic!(\"expected User message\"),\n        }\n    }\n}"
```json
}
```

> AGENT

22 files changed — all expected. Here's a summary of the implementation:

**Core type change** (`crates/agents/src/model.rs`):
- Added `name: Option<String>` field to `ChatMessage::User`
- Updated `user()` and `user_multimodal()` constructors to set `name: None`
- Added `user_named()` and `user_multimodal_named()` constructors
- Updated `to_openai_value()` to include `"name"` in JSON when present
- Updated `values_to_chat_messages()` to extract `sender_name`/`username` from persisted `channel` metadata
- Added 6 unit tests for sender name functionality

**Anthropic provider** (`crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs`):
- Prepends `[name]: ` to user content text when name is present (Anthropic doesn't support `name` field natively)

**Current message path** (`streaming.rs`, `run_with_tools.rs`, `send.rs`):
- Threaded `sender_name: Option<String>` through `run_streaming()` → message construction
- Threaded through `run_with_tools()` → `run_agent_loop_streaming()` → message construction
- Extracted sender name from `params["channel"]` in `send.rs`

**Pattern match fixes** (14 files):
- Added `..` or `name: _` to all `ChatMessage::User` destructuring patterns
- Added `name: None` to all `ChatMessage::User` constructions
